- **certainty**: 五维置信度评分与 HITL 决策框架（语言无关） ([Marcher-lam/STDD-COPILOT](https://github.com/Marcher-lam/STDD-COPILOT/tree/HEAD/src/templates/skills/stdd/certainty) · ★3)
- **certainty-audit**: A proof is a physical process resting on an assumption, not a separate category of truth above ordinary explanation. Use when: Someone ends a debate by saying the math proves it, the model is rigorous, or the numbers do not lie. ([adamtpang/summon.guide](https://github.com/adamtpang/summon.guide/tree/HEAD/plugins/deutsch/skills/certainty-audit))
- **certainty-closer**: Canonical operating manual. Any agent with shell + file access and this file can take certainty-closer from clone → dry-run → live. For the fast path, see **[QUICKSTART.md](./QUICKSTART.md)**. ([limitless-labs-group/agents-starter](https://github.com/limitless-labs-group/agents-starter/tree/HEAD/src/strategies/certainty-closer) · ★6)
- **certainty-grade**: Apply HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW certainty grading to all agent findings and recommendations. Use to gate human review, auto-fix, or autonomous action. ([elasticdotventures/_b00t_](https://github.com/elasticdotventures/_b00t_/tree/HEAD/plugins/next-task/skills/certainty-grade) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **certainty-illusion**: Knowledge base from "The Certainty Illusion: What You Don't Know and Why It Matters" by Timothy Caulfield. Use when applying Caulfield's frameworks for spotting scienceploitation and science hype, evaluating studies and predatory or hijacked journals, decoding health halos and goodness marketing, judging online reviews, ratings and polls, and the Certainty Six; studying the book; or referencing its concepts. ([JoKerDii/ai-maintained-book-knowledge-base](https://github.com/JoKerDii/ai-maintained-book-knowledge-base/tree/HEAD/book-skills/certainty-illusion))
- **certainty-score-threshold-optimization**: Use when MASI-annotated query data contains cell types absent from the reference (e.g., Oetjen et al. 16 cell types vs. 12-type 10x PBMC reference) and you must recover the unseen types by tuning the certainty-score threshold in the 0.5–0.6 range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/certainty-score-threshold-optimization))
